Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the magnetics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 99. The resulting operator achieves bound O(872/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
